#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");
    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, \"MatrixC\");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+
+// A block computes a 64x64 output tile. Each thread keeps a 4x4 tile in
+// registers, and all threads reuse the same input tiles in shared memory.
+template <bool aligned>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[tileSize][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[tileDepth][tileSize];
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileSize;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileSize;
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+            const unsigned aRow = (tid + p * 256) / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned aCol = (tid + p * 256) % tileDepth;
+            const unsigned bRow = (tid + p * 256) / tileSize;
+            const unsigned bCol = (tid + p * 256) % tileSize;
+            tileA[aRow][aCol] = (aligned || (rowBase + aRow < rows && base + aCol < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + aRow) * N + base + aCol] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bRow][bCol] = (aligned || (base + bRow < N && colBase + bCol < N))
+                ? B[(base + bRow) * N + colBase + bCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+                a[p] = tileA[ty + p * 16][k];
+                b[p] = tileB[k][tx + p * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+                    // Keep the original double-precision multiply/add rounding
+                    // and increasing-k accumulation order (no reduced precision).
+                    sums[i][j] = __dadd_rn(sums[i][j], __dmul_rn(a[i], b[j]));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + i * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * 16;
+            if (aligned || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
+class CudaMatmul {
+    struct Device {
+        int id = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* a = nullptr;
+        double* b = nullptr;
+        double* c = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    size_t n;
+    std::vector<Device> devices;
+    double* pinnedOutput = nullptr;
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (auto& device : devices) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.id);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream);
+            if (device.a) cudaFree(device.a);
+            if (device.b) cudaFree(device.b);
+            if (device.c) cudaFree(device.c);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+        }
+        if (pinnedOutput) cudaHostUnregister(pinnedOutput);
+    }
+
+public:
+    // Setup and input uploads are outside the computation timer. The output is
+    // pinned so row slabs can be downloaded concurrently from different GPUs.
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : n(N) {
+        try {
+            int deviceCount = 0;
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Get CUDA device count");
+            if (deviceCount == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+            cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Get CUDA device properties");
+            const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
+            // Give each GPU enough blocks to occupy its multiprocessors. This
+            // avoids spreading small problems over otherwise idle devices.
+            const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1,
+                tiles * tiles / (2 * static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount)));
+            const size_t count = std::min({static_cast<size_t>(deviceCount), usefulDevices, tiles});
+            devices.resize(count);
+
+            checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(C.data(), C.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                      "Pin result buffer");
+            pinnedOutput = C.data();
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                auto& device = devices[i];
+                device.id = static_cast<int>(i);
+                device.firstRow = (tiles * i / count) * tileSize;
+                const size_t end = std::min(n, (tiles * (i + 1) / count) * tileSize);
+                device.rows = end - device.firstRow;
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "Create CUDA stream");
+                const size_t slabBytes = device.rows * n * sizeof(double);
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.a), slabBytes), "Allocate device A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.b), B.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                          "Allocate device B");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.c), slabBytes), "Allocate device C");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.a, A.data() + device.firstRow * n, slabBytes,
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.b, B.data(), B.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload B");
+            }
+            for (const auto& device : devices) {
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish input uploads");
+            }
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatmul(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    CudaMatmul& operator=(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    ~CudaMatmul() { release(); }
+
+    void multiply(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        // Submit every GPU's work before waiting for any of them.
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            const dim3 block(16, 16);
+            const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize),
+                            static_cast<unsigned>((device.rows + tileSize - 1) / tileSize));
+            if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+                multiplyTiles<true><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+            } else {
+                multiplyTiles<false><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * n, device.c,
+                                      device.rows * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
+                                      device.stream), "Download result");
+        }
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+};

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+
+// A block computes a 64x64 output tile. Each thread keeps a 4x4 tile in
+// registers, and all threads reuse the same input tiles in shared memory.
+template <bool aligned>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[tileSize][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[tileDepth][tileSize];
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileSize;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileSize;
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+            const unsigned aRow = (tid + p * 256) / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned aCol = (tid + p * 256) % tileDepth;
+            const unsigned bRow = (tid + p * 256) / tileSize;
+            const unsigned bCol = (tid + p * 256) % tileSize;
+            tileA[aRow][aCol] = (aligned || (rowBase + aRow < rows && base + aCol < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + aRow) * N + base + aCol] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bRow][bCol] = (aligned || (base + bRow < N && colBase + bCol < N))
+                ? B[(base + bRow) * N + colBase + bCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+                a[p] = tileA[ty + p * 16][k];
+                b[p] = tileB[k][tx + p * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+                    // Keep the original double-precision multiply/add rounding
+                    // and increasing-k accumulation order (no reduced precision).
+                    sums[i][j] = __dadd_rn(sums[i][j], __dmul_rn(a[i], b[j]));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + i * 16;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * 16;
+            if (aligned || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
+class CudaMatmul {
+    struct Device {
+        int id = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* a = nullptr;
+        double* b = nullptr;
+        double* c = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    size_t n;
+    std::vector<Device> devices;
+    double* pinnedOutput = nullptr;
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (auto& device : devices) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.id);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream);
+            if (device.a) cudaFree(device.a);
+            if (device.b) cudaFree(device.b);
+            if (device.c) cudaFree(device.c);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+        }
+        if (pinnedOutput) cudaHostUnregister(pinnedOutput);
+    }
+
+public:
+    // Setup and input uploads are outside the computation timer. The output is
+    // pinned so row slabs can be downloaded concurrently from different GPUs.
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : n(N) {
+        try {
+            int deviceCount = 0;
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Get CUDA device count");
+            if (deviceCount == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+            cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Get CUDA device properties");
+            const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
+            // Give each GPU enough blocks to occupy its multiprocessors. This
+            // avoids spreading small problems over otherwise idle devices.
+            const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1,
+                tiles * tiles / (2 * static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount)));
+            const size_t count = std::min({static_cast<size_t>(deviceCount), usefulDevices, tiles});
+            devices.resize(count);
+
+            checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(C.data(), C.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                      "Pin result buffer");
+            pinnedOutput = C.data();
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                auto& device = devices[i];
+                device.id = static_cast<int>(i);
+                device.firstRow = (tiles * i / count) * tileSize;
+                const size_t end = std::min(n, (tiles * (i + 1) / count) * tileSize);
+                device.rows = end - device.firstRow;
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "Create CUDA stream");
+                const size_t slabBytes = device.rows * n * sizeof(double);
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.a), slabBytes), "Allocate device A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.b), B.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                          "Allocate device B");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.c), slabBytes), "Allocate device C");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.a, A.data() + device.firstRow * n, slabBytes,
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.b, B.data(), B.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload B");
+            }
+            for (const auto& device : devices) {
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish input uploads");
+            }
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatmul(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    CudaMatmul& operator=(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    ~CudaMatmul() { release(); }
+
+    void multiply(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        // Submit every GPU's work before waiting for any of them.
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            const dim3 block(16, 16);
+            const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize),
+                            static_cast<unsigned>((device.rows + tileSize - 1) / tileSize));
+            if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+                multiplyTiles<true><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+            } else {
+                multiplyTiles<false><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+            }
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * n, device.c,
+                                      device.rows * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
+                                      device.stream), "Download result");
+        }
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {

+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+
+// Each thread keeps a 4x4 output tile in registers, and all threads reuse
+// input tiles in shared memory. Shorter row tiles expose more blocks for
+// small matrices; large matrices use 64x64 tiles for greater input reuse.
+template <bool aligned, unsigned tileRows>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[tileDepth][tileSize];
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    constexpr unsigned rowStride = tileRows / 4;
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 16 * rowStride;
+    const unsigned tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileSize;
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+            const unsigned aRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned aCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileDepth;
+            tileA[aRow][aCol] = (aligned || (rowBase + aRow < rows && base + aCol < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + aRow) * N + base + aCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < tileDepth * tileSize / threads; ++p) {
+            const unsigned bRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileSize;
+            const unsigned bCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileSize;
+            tileB[bRow][bCol] = (aligned || (base + bRow < N && colBase + bCol < N))
+                ? B[(base + bRow) * N + colBase + bCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+                a[p] = tileA[ty + p * rowStride][k];
+                b[p] = tileB[k][tx + p * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+                    // Keep the original double-precision multiply/add rounding
+                    // and increasing-k accumulation order (no reduced precision).
+                    sums[i][j] = __dadd_rn(sums[i][j], __dmul_rn(a[i], b[j]));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + i * rowStride;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * 16;
+            if (aligned || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+class CudaMatmul {
+    struct Device {
+        int id = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* a = nullptr;
+        double* b = nullptr;
+        double* c = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    size_t n;
+    size_t rowTile;
+    std::vector<Device> devices;
+    double* pinnedOutput = nullptr;
+
+    template <unsigned tileRows>
+    void launch(const Device& device) {
+        const dim3 block(16, tileRows / 4);
+        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize),
+                        static_cast<unsigned>((device.rows + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<true, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<false, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    template <unsigned tileRows>
+    void prepareKernel() {
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes{};
+        // Load the kernel before timing, including any driver-side PTX JIT.
+        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<true, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<false, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (auto& device : devices) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.id);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream);
+            if (device.a) cudaFree(device.a);
+            if (device.b) cudaFree(device.b);
+            if (device.c) cudaFree(device.c);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+        }
+        if (pinnedOutput) cudaHostUnregister(pinnedOutput);
+    }
+
+public:
+    // Setup and input uploads are outside the computation timer. The output is
+    // pinned so row slabs can be downloaded concurrently from different GPUs.
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : n(N), rowTile(N < 1024 ? 16 : tileSize) {
+        try {
+            int deviceCount = 0;
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Get CUDA device count");
+            if (deviceCount == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+            cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Get CUDA device properties");
+            const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
+            // Give each GPU enough blocks to occupy its multiprocessors. This
+            // avoids spreading small problems over otherwise idle devices.
+            const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1,
+                tiles * tiles / (2 * static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount)));
+            const size_t rowTiles = (n + rowTile - 1) / rowTile;
+            const size_t count = std::min({static_cast<size_t>(deviceCount), usefulDevices, rowTiles});
+            devices.resize(count);
+
+            checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(C.data(), C.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                      "Pin result buffer");
+            pinnedOutput = C.data();
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                auto& device = devices[i];
+                device.id = static_cast<int>(i);
+                device.firstRow = (rowTiles * i / count) * rowTile;
+                const size_t end = std::min(n, (rowTiles * (i + 1) / count) * rowTile);
+                device.rows = end - device.firstRow;
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "Create CUDA stream");
+                const size_t slabBytes = device.rows * n * sizeof(double);
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.a), slabBytes), "Allocate device A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.b), B.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                          "Allocate device B");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.c), slabBytes), "Allocate device C");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.a, A.data() + device.firstRow * n, slabBytes,
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.b, B.data(), B.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload B");
+                if (rowTile == 16) prepareKernel<16>();
+                else prepareKernel<tileSize>();
+            }
+            for (const auto& device : devices) {
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish input uploads");
+            }
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatmul(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    CudaMatmul& operator=(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    ~CudaMatmul() { release(); }
+
+    void multiply(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        // Submit every GPU's work before waiting for any of them.
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (rowTile == 16) launch<16>(device);
+            else launch<tileSize>(device);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * n, device.c,
+                                      device.rows * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
+                                      device.stream), "Download result");
+        }
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace

+template <bool aligned, unsigned tileRows>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[tileDepth][tileSize];

+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileSize;
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+

+        #pragma unroll

+            }
+        } catch (...) {

+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatmul(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    CudaMatmul& operator=(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    ~CudaMatmul() { release(); }
+
+    void multiply(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        // Submit every GPU's work before waiting for any of them.
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (rowTile == 16) launch<16>(device);
+            else launch<tileSize>(device);

+                                      device.rows * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
+                                      device.stream), "Download result");
+        }
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+
+// Each thread keeps a 4x4 output tile in registers, and all threads reuse
+// input tiles in shared memory. Shorter row tiles expose more blocks for
+// small matrices; large matrices use 64x64 tiles for greater input reuse.
+template <bool aligned, unsigned tileRows>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[tileDepth][tileSize];
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    constexpr unsigned rowStride = tileRows / 4;
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 16 * rowStride;
+    const unsigned tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileSize;
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+            const unsigned aRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned aCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileDepth;
+            tileA[aRow][aCol] = (aligned || (rowBase + aRow < rows && base + aCol < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + aRow) * N + base + aCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < tileDepth * tileSize / threads; ++p) {
+            const unsigned bRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileSize;
+            const unsigned bCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileSize;
+            tileB[bRow][bCol] = (aligned || (base + bRow < N && colBase + bCol < N))
+                ? B[(base + bRow) * N + colBase + bCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+                a[p] = tileA[ty + p * rowStride][k];
+                b[p] = tileB[k][tx + p * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+                    // Keep the original double-precision multiply/add rounding
+                    // and increasing-k accumulation order (no reduced precision).
+                    sums[i][j] = __dadd_rn(sums[i][j], __dmul_rn(a[i], b[j]));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + i * rowStride;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * 16;
+            if (aligned || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
+class CudaMatmul {
+    struct Device {
+        int id = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* a = nullptr;
+        double* b = nullptr;
+        double* c = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    size_t n;
+    size_t rowTile;
+    std::vector<Device> devices;
+    double* pinnedOutput = nullptr;
+
+    template <unsigned tileRows>
+    void launch(const Device& device) {
+        const dim3 block(16, tileRows / 4);
+        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize),
+                        static_cast<unsigned>((device.rows + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<true, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<false, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    template <unsigned tileRows>
+    void prepareKernel() {
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes{};
+        // Load the kernel before timing, including any driver-side PTX JIT.
+        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<true, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<false, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (auto& device : devices) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.id);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream);
+            if (device.a) cudaFree(device.a);
+            if (device.b) cudaFree(device.b);
+            if (device.c) cudaFree(device.c);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+        }
+        if (pinnedOutput) cudaHostUnregister(pinnedOutput);
+    }
+
+public:
+    // Setup and input uploads are outside the computation timer. The output is
+    // pinned so row slabs can be downloaded concurrently from different GPUs.
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : n(N), rowTile(N < 1024 ? 16 : tileSize) {
+        try {
+            int deviceCount = 0;
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Get CUDA device count");
+            if (deviceCount == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+            cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Get CUDA device properties");
+            const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
+            // Give each GPU enough blocks to occupy its multiprocessors. This
+            // avoids spreading small problems over otherwise idle devices.
+            const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1,
+                tiles * tiles / (2 * static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount)));
+            const size_t rowTiles = (n + rowTile - 1) / rowTile;
+            const size_t count = std::min({static_cast<size_t>(deviceCount), usefulDevices, rowTiles});
+            devices.resize(count);
+
+            checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(C.data(), C.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                      "Pin result buffer");
+            pinnedOutput = C.data();
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                auto& device = devices[i];
+                device.id = static_cast<int>(i);
+                device.firstRow = (rowTiles * i / count) * rowTile;
+                const size_t end = std::min(n, (rowTiles * (i + 1) / count) * rowTile);
+                device.rows = end - device.firstRow;
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "Create CUDA stream");
+                const size_t slabBytes = device.rows * n * sizeof(double);
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.a), slabBytes), "Allocate device A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.b), B.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                          "Allocate device B");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.c), slabBytes), "Allocate device C");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.a, A.data() + device.firstRow * n, slabBytes,
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.b, B.data(), B.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload B");
+                if (rowTile == 16) prepareKernel<16>();
+                else prepareKernel<tileSize>();
+            }
+            for (const auto& device : devices) {
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish input uploads");
+            }
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatmul(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    CudaMatmul& operator=(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    ~CudaMatmul() { release(); }
+
+    void multiply(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        // Submit every GPU's work before waiting for any of them.
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (rowTile == 16) launch<16>(device);
+            else launch<tileSize>(device);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * n, device.c,
+                                      device.rows * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
+                                      device.stream), "Download result");
+        }
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace

+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));

+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+
+// Each thread keeps a 4x4 output tile in registers, and all threads reuse
+// input tiles in shared memory. Shorter row tiles expose more blocks for
+// small matrices; large matrices use 64x64 tiles for greater input reuse.
+template <bool aligned, unsigned tileRows>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[tileDepth][tileSize];
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    constexpr unsigned rowStride = tileRows / 4;
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 16 * rowStride;
+    const unsigned tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileSize;
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+            const unsigned aRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned aCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileDepth;
+            tileA[aRow][aCol] = (aligned || (rowBase + aRow < rows && base + aCol < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + aRow) * N + base + aCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < tileDepth * tileSize / threads; ++p) {
+            const unsigned bRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileSize;
+            const unsigned bCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileSize;
+            tileB[bRow][bCol] = (aligned || (base + bRow < N && colBase + bCol < N))
+                ? B[(base + bRow) * N + colBase + bCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+                a[p] = tileA[ty + p * rowStride][k];
+                b[p] = tileB[k][tx + p * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+                    // Keep the original double-precision multiply/add rounding
+                    // and increasing-k accumulation order (no reduced precision).
+                    sums[i][j] = __dadd_rn(sums[i][j], __dmul_rn(a[i], b[j]));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + i * rowStride;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * 16;
+            if (aligned || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

+class CudaMatmul {
+    struct Device {
+        int id = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* a = nullptr;
+        double* b = nullptr;
+        double* c = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    size_t n;
+    size_t rowTile;
+    std::vector<Device> devices;
+    double* pinnedOutput = nullptr;
+
+    template <unsigned tileRows>
+    void launch(const Device& device) {
+        const dim3 block(16, tileRows / 4);
+        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize),
+                        static_cast<unsigned>((device.rows + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<true, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<false, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    template <unsigned tileRows>
+    void prepareKernel() {
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes{};
+        // Load the kernel before timing, including any driver-side PTX JIT.
+        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<true, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<false, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (auto& device : devices) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.id);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream);
+            if (device.a) cudaFree(device.a);
+            if (device.b) cudaFree(device.b);
+            if (device.c) cudaFree(device.c);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+        }
+        if (pinnedOutput) cudaHostUnregister(pinnedOutput);
+    }
+
+public:
+    // Setup and input uploads are outside the computation timer. The output is
+    // pinned so row slabs can be downloaded concurrently from different GPUs.
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : n(N), rowTile(N < 1024 ? 16 : tileSize) {
+        try {
+            int deviceCount = 0;
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Get CUDA device count");
+            if (deviceCount == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+            cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Get CUDA device properties");
+            const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
+            // Give each GPU enough blocks to occupy its multiprocessors. This
+            // avoids spreading small problems over otherwise idle devices.
+            const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1,
+                tiles * tiles / (2 * static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount)));
+            const size_t rowTiles = (n + rowTile - 1) / rowTile;
+            const size_t count = std::min({static_cast<size_t>(deviceCount), usefulDevices, rowTiles});
+            devices.resize(count);
+
+            checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(C.data(), C.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                      "Pin result buffer");
+            pinnedOutput = C.data();
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                auto& device = devices[i];
+                device.id = static_cast<int>(i);
+                device.firstRow = (rowTiles * i / count) * rowTile;
+                const size_t end = std::min(n, (rowTiles * (i + 1) / count) * rowTile);
+                device.rows = end - device.firstRow;
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "Create CUDA stream");
+                const size_t slabBytes = device.rows * n * sizeof(double);
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.a), slabBytes), "Allocate device A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.b), B.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                          "Allocate device B");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.c), slabBytes), "Allocate device C");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.a, A.data() + device.firstRow * n, slabBytes,
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.b, B.data(), B.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload B");
+                if (rowTile == 16) prepareKernel<16>();
+                else prepareKernel<tileSize>();
+            }
+            for (const auto& device : devices) {
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish input uploads");
+            }
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatmul(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    CudaMatmul& operator=(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    ~CudaMatmul() { release(); }
+
+    void multiply(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        // Submit every GPU's work before waiting for any of them.
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (rowTile == 16) launch<16>(device);
+            else launch<tileSize>(device);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * n, device.c,
+                                      device.rows * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
+                                      device.stream), "Download result");
+        }
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+};
+

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

namespace {

void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
    }
}

constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;

// Each thread keeps a 4x4 output tile in registers, and all threads reuse
// input tiles in shared memory. Shorter row tiles expose more blocks for
// small matrices; large matrices use 64x64 tiles for greater input reuse.
template <bool aligned, unsigned tileRows>
__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
                              const double* __restrict__ B,
                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
    __shared__ double tileA[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
    __shared__ double tileB[tileDepth][tileSize];
    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
    constexpr unsigned rowStride = tileRows / 4;
    constexpr unsigned threads = 16 * rowStride;
    const unsigned tid = ty * 16 + tx;
    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileSize;
    double sums[4][4] = {};

    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
            const unsigned aRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileDepth;
            const unsigned aCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileDepth;
            tileA[aRow][aCol] = (aligned || (rowBase + aRow < rows && base + aCol < N))
                ? A[(rowBase + aRow) * N + base + aCol] : 0.0;
        }
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned p = 0; p < tileDepth * tileSize / threads; ++p) {
            const unsigned bRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileSize;
            const unsigned bCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileSize;
            tileB[bRow][bCol] = (aligned || (base + bRow < N && colBase + bCol < N))
                ? B[(base + bRow) * N + colBase + bCol] : 0.0;
        }
        __syncthreads();

        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
            double a[4], b[4];
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
                a[p] = tileA[ty + p * rowStride][k];
                b[p] = tileB[k][tx + p * 16];
            }
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
                #pragma unroll
                for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
                    // Keep the original double-precision multiply/add rounding
                    // and increasing-k accumulation order (no reduced precision).
                    sums[i][j] = __dadd_rn(sums[i][j], __dmul_rn(a[i], b[j]));
                }
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }

    #pragma unroll
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + i * rowStride;
            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * 16;
            if (aligned || (row < rows && col < N)) {
                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
            }
        }
    }
}

class CudaMatmul {
    struct Device {
        int id = 0;
        size_t firstRow = 0;
        size_t rows = 0;
        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
        double* a = nullptr;
        double* b = nullptr;
        double* c = nullptr;
    };

    size_t n;
    size_t rowTile;
    std::vector<Device> devices;
    double* pinnedOutput = nullptr;

    template <unsigned tileRows>
    void launch(const Device& device) {
        const dim3 block(16, tileRows / 4);
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize),
                        static_cast<unsigned>((device.rows + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
            multiplyTiles<true, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
        } else {
            multiplyTiles<false, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch matrix multiplication");
    }

    template <unsigned tileRows>
    void prepareKernel() {
        cudaFuncAttributes attributes{};
        // Load the kernel before timing, including any driver-side PTX JIT.
        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<true, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
        } else {
            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<false, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
        }
    }

    void release() noexcept {
        for (auto& device : devices) {
            cudaSetDevice(device.id);
            if (device.stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream);
            if (device.a) cudaFree(device.a);
            if (device.b) cudaFree(device.b);
            if (device.c) cudaFree(device.c);
            if (device.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
        }
        if (pinnedOutput) cudaHostUnregister(pinnedOutput);
    }

public:
    // Setup and input uploads are outside the computation timer. The output is
    // pinned so row slabs can be downloaded concurrently from different GPUs.
    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : n(N), rowTile(N < 1024 ? 16 : tileSize) {
        try {
            int deviceCount = 0;
            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Get CUDA device count");
            if (deviceCount == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");

            cudaDeviceProp properties{};
            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Get CUDA device properties");
            const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
            // Give each GPU enough blocks to occupy its multiprocessors. This
            // avoids spreading small problems over otherwise idle devices.
            const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1,
                tiles * tiles / (2 * static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount)));
            const size_t rowTiles = (n + rowTile - 1) / rowTile;
            const size_t count = std::min({static_cast<size_t>(deviceCount), usefulDevices, rowTiles});
            devices.resize(count);

            checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(C.data(), C.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterPortable),
                      "Pin result buffer");
            pinnedOutput = C.data();
            for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
                auto& device = devices[i];
                device.id = static_cast<int>(i);
                device.firstRow = (rowTiles * i / count) * rowTile;
                const size_t end = std::min(n, (rowTiles * (i + 1) / count) * rowTile);
                device.rows = end - device.firstRow;
                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
                          "Create CUDA stream");
                const size_t slabBytes = device.rows * n * sizeof(double);
                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.a), slabBytes), "Allocate device A");
                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.b), B.size() * sizeof(double)),
                          "Allocate device B");
                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.c), slabBytes), "Allocate device C");
                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.a, A.data() + device.firstRow * n, slabBytes,
                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload A");
                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.b, B.data(), B.size() * sizeof(double),
                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload B");
                if (rowTile == 16) prepareKernel<16>();
                else prepareKernel<tileSize>();
            }
            for (const auto& device : devices) {
                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish input uploads");
            }
        } catch (...) {
            release();
            throw;
        }
    }

    CudaMatmul(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
    CudaMatmul& operator=(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
    ~CudaMatmul() { release(); }

    void multiply(std::vector<double>& C) {
        // Submit every GPU's work before waiting for any of them.
        for (const auto& device : devices) {
            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
            if (rowTile == 16) launch<16>(device);
            else launch<tileSize>(device);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * n, device.c,
                                      device.rows * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
                                      device.stream), "Download result");
        }
        for (const auto& device : devices) {
            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish matrix multiplication");
        }
    }
};

} // namespace

// Recompute representative elements on the CPU, including boundary rows.
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    const size_t checkPoints[] = {0, N / 4, N / 2, 3 * N / 4, N - 1};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (!std::isfinite(actual) || relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) try {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const char* value = argv[++i];
            char* end = nullptr;
            const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(value, &end, 10);
            if (value[0] == '-' || end == value || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
                parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
                fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size must be a positive integer\n");
                return 1;
            }
            N = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size is too large\n");
        return 1;
    }

    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    CudaMatmul multiplication(A, B, C, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    
    multiplication.multiply(C);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", static_cast<long long>(duration.count()));
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count() / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
} catch (const std::exception& error) {
    fprintf(stderr, "Error: %s\n", error.what());
    return 1;
}

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr size_t tileSize = 64;
+constexpr unsigned tileDepth = 16;
+
+// Each thread keeps a 4x4 output tile in registers, and all threads reuse
+// input tiles in shared memory. Shorter row tiles expose more blocks for
+// small matrices; large matrices use 64x64 tiles for greater input reuse.
+template <bool aligned, unsigned tileRows>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[tileRows][tileDepth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[tileDepth][tileSize];
+    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned ty = threadIdx.y;
+    constexpr unsigned rowStride = tileRows / 4;
+    constexpr unsigned threads = 16 * rowStride;
+    const unsigned tid = ty * 16 + tx;
+    const size_t rowBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * tileRows;
+    const size_t colBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * tileSize;
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += tileDepth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+            const unsigned aRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileDepth;
+            const unsigned aCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileDepth;
+            tileA[aRow][aCol] = (aligned || (rowBase + aRow < rows && base + aCol < N))
+                ? A[(rowBase + aRow) * N + base + aCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned p = 0; p < tileDepth * tileSize / threads; ++p) {
+            const unsigned bRow = (tid + p * threads) / tileSize;
+            const unsigned bCol = (tid + p * threads) % tileSize;
+            tileB[bRow][bCol] = (aligned || (base + bRow < N && colBase + bCol < N))
+                ? B[(base + bRow) * N + colBase + bCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned k = 0; k < tileDepth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {
+                a[p] = tileA[ty + p * rowStride][k];
+                b[p] = tileB[k][tx + p * 16];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+                    // Keep the original double-precision multiply/add rounding
+                    // and increasing-k accumulation order (no reduced precision).
+                    sums[i][j] = __dadd_rn(sums[i][j], __dmul_rn(a[i], b[j]));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
+            const size_t row = rowBase + ty + i * rowStride;
+            const size_t col = colBase + tx + j * 16;
+            if (aligned || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+class CudaMatmul {
+    struct Device {
+        int id = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        double* a = nullptr;
+        double* b = nullptr;
+        double* c = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    size_t n;
+    size_t rowTile;
+    std::vector<Device> devices;
+    double* pinnedOutput = nullptr;
+
+    template <unsigned tileRows>
+    void launch(const Device& device) {
+        const dim3 block(16, tileRows / 4);
+        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize),
+                        static_cast<unsigned>((device.rows + tileRows - 1) / tileRows));
+        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<true, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<false, tileRows><<<grid, block, 0, device.stream>>>(device.a, device.b, device.c, n, device.rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    template <unsigned tileRows>
+    void prepareKernel() {
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes{};
+        // Load the kernel before timing, including any driver-side PTX JIT.
+        if (n % tileSize == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<true, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<false, tileRows>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (auto& device : devices) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.id);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream);
+            if (device.a) cudaFree(device.a);
+            if (device.b) cudaFree(device.b);
+            if (device.c) cudaFree(device.c);
+            if (device.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+        }
+        if (pinnedOutput) cudaHostUnregister(pinnedOutput);
+    }
+
+public:
+    // Setup and input uploads are outside the computation timer. The output is
+    // pinned so row slabs can be downloaded concurrently from different GPUs.
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : n(N), rowTile(N < 1024 ? 16 : tileSize) {
+        try {
+            int deviceCount = 0;
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Get CUDA device count");
+            if (deviceCount == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+            cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+            checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Get CUDA device properties");
+            const size_t tiles = (n + tileSize - 1) / tileSize;
+            // Give each GPU enough blocks to occupy its multiprocessors. This
+            // avoids spreading small problems over otherwise idle devices.
+            const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1,
+                tiles * tiles / (2 * static_cast<size_t>(properties.multiProcessorCount)));
+            const size_t rowTiles = (n + rowTile - 1) / rowTile;
+            const size_t count = std::min({static_cast<size_t>(deviceCount), usefulDevices, rowTiles});
+            devices.resize(count);
+
+            checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(C.data(), C.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                      "Pin result buffer");
+            pinnedOutput = C.data();
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                auto& device = devices[i];
+                device.id = static_cast<int>(i);
+                device.firstRow = (rowTiles * i / count) * rowTile;
+                const size_t end = std::min(n, (rowTiles * (i + 1) / count) * rowTile);
+                device.rows = end - device.firstRow;
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "Create CUDA stream");
+                const size_t slabBytes = device.rows * n * sizeof(double);
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.a), slabBytes), "Allocate device A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.b), B.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                          "Allocate device B");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.c), slabBytes), "Allocate device C");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.a, A.data() + device.firstRow * n, slabBytes,
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload A");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.b, B.data(), B.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream), "Upload B");
+                if (rowTile == 16) prepareKernel<16>();
+                else prepareKernel<tileSize>();
+            }
+            for (const auto& device : devices) {
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish input uploads");
+            }
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatmul(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    CudaMatmul& operator=(const CudaMatmul&) = delete;
+    ~CudaMatmul() { release(); }
+
+    void multiply(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        // Submit every GPU's work before waiting for any of them.
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (rowTile == 16) launch<16>(device);
+            else launch<tileSize>(device);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * n, device.c,
+                                      device.rows * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,
+                                      device.stream), "Download result");
+        }
+        for (const auto& device : devices) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.id), "Select CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "Finish matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+};
